pāṇṭiyar mīṉ koṭi
nāṉ ilaṅkaiyil irukkum poḻutu, pāṇṭiyaṉ koṭi eppaṭi irukkum eṉṟu cintittēṉ. oru ciṅkaḷa naṇpar eṉakku oru paḻaiya pāṇṭiyar koṭiyaik kāṭṭiṉār. atai nāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ koṭi eṉṟu ēṟṟukkoḷḷavillai. kāraṇam ciṅkaḷavaṉukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ koṭiyaip paṟṟi eṉṉa teriyum? - nāṉ tamiḻaṉ, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭāṉ. koṟkaikku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa ūril piṟantavaṉ eṉṟa āṇavamē yākum. appāl cila kālam kaḻittu nāṉ paḻaiya kācukaḷaip paṟṟi āynta poḻutu kōṭriṅṭaṉ eṉṉum oru kācu āyvu nipuṇar, tam nūlil iḷavaracaṉ caṭavarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ ilaṅkaiyai veṉṟa tāka urimai pārāṭṭukiṟāṉ. caṭavarmaṉ vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ II īḻa nāṭṭai veṉṟu (ki.pi. 1253/4-1275) oru ilaṅkai maṉṉaṉaik koṉṟu avaṉ paṭaiyaiyum, tēraiyum, karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyum aracak kaṭṭilaiyum maṇi muṭiyaiyum, ceṅkōlaiyum kaḻuttu mālaikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum kavarntu koṇṭu taṉatu iraṭṭai mīṉkaḷ poṟitta mīṉ koṭiyait tirukkōṇa malaiyil poṟit tāṉ eṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1
nāṉ itaip paṭittumuṭittum maṟunāḷē īḻa nāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamāy viḷaṅkum tirukkōṇa malaikkup pōy, malaiyeṅkum pāṇṭiyaṉ koṭiyai tēṭi alaintēṉ. piṉ oru naṇpariṉ utaviyāl tirukkōṇa malaimītu poṟikkappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ mīṉ koṭiyaik kaṇṭēṉ. itu pāṇṭiya aracaṉāl poṟitta uṇmaiyāṉakoṭi eṉṟu uḷam pūrittēṉ. piṟkālattil iṭaccukkārarkaḷ akkoṭiyaip poṟittuḷḷa pāṟaiyai appaṭiyē uṭaittu, taṅkaḷiṉ tūya peraṭarik kōṭṭai eṉṟa araṇmītu vaittukkaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atai oḷip paṭam eṭuttu vantēṉ. appaṭam iṅku veḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ vaṭaintiyāvai veṉṟu imayattiṉ mītu kuṭaiyai nāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟum īḻattaiveṉṟu kōṇa malai mītu iṇaikkayalkaḷ tīṭṭiya koṭiyaip poṟittāṉ eṉṟum tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ maṉṉarkaḷukkup pukaḻmālai cūṭṭuvatu uṇṭu. antap pākkaḷil ellām iṇaikkayalkaḷ eṉṟē kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēlum varalāṟṟu ārāyccip pēraṟiñar īrācu aṭika ḷārum tam āyvuk kaṇkoṇṭu naṉku āyntu pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ ciṉṉam iru mīṉkaḷē eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.2 atōṭu ulakil uḷḷa pala nāṭukaḷilum vāḻum tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷ mīṉai vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. atēpōl paṇṭaiyat tamiḻakattil uḷḷa paratavarkaḷum cuṟā mīṉ kompai naṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tam koṭiyil iru mīṉkaḷ poṟittiruppatu iru kaṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkēyām. attuṭaṉ mīṉkaḷ ceṅkuttāy niṟpatupōl tīṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ valimaiyaik kāṭṭuvataṟkēyākum.
muttiraikaḷ
pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ conta muttiraiyil ceṅkuttāy niṟkum iru mīṉkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭu naṭuvil viḷakkup pōṉṟa oru uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. aracu campantappaṭṭa potu muttiraikaḷil tamiḻ aracu eṉpataik kāṭṭum muṟaiyil iru mīṉkaḷ ceṅkuttāy niṟkum, arukē puli, vil uruvam mutaliya tīṭṭap peṟṟirukkum. cila muttiraikaḷil kīḻē villaip poṟittu ataṉ mītu iṇaik kayalum puliyum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṇṭiyarkaḷ oru kōyilukku aḷitta māṉiyattaik kuṟippiṭum ceppēṭṭil uḷḷa muttiraiyil mīṉukku mēlē kuṭaiyum arukē cāmaraṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cōḻarkaḷ aḷitta cācaṉaṅkaḷil puliyiṉ mēlē kuṭaiyum irumaruṅkiṉum iru kavari kaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kācukaḷ cilavaṟṟil vil, mīṉkaḷ, puli ākiya uruvaṅkaḷaip poṟittu iru puṟaṅkaḷilum tiruviḷakkukaḷ tikaḻumāṟu ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
eṉṉiṭam 10 muttiraikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittilum - cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷāy iruntālum pāṇṭiyar ceppēṭu kaḷāy iruntālum avaikaḷil ellām tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉum mūvar tam araca ciṉṉaṅkaḷum tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku vēṟōr iṭattil iru maṉṉarkaḷiṉ iru cācaṉaṅkaḷiṉ uruvap paṭaṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

13. maṉṉarum piṉṉarum
koṟkai, pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉ paḻamperum kōnakaram, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ paṇpu vāynta tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉam; iṭaic caṅkam nilaviya mūtūr; perum pulamai vāynta pulavarkaḷum pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷum oruṅkiruntu muttamiḻ vaḷartta iṭaic caṅkam nilaviya pērūr. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āyiram āyiram kal tolaiviṟku appāliruntu kirēkkarkaḷum, yavaṉarkaḷum urōmarkaḷum cāvakarkaḷum ciṅkaḷavarkaḷum pīṉiciyarkaḷum ekiptiyarkaḷum papilōṉiyarkaḷum vaṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup periya marakkalaṅkaḷil vantiṟaṅkiya pāṇṭiya maṉṉar kaḷiṉ akanakarāyum puṟanakarāyum koḷḷappaṭṭa mānakarākum. potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum ciṟappākap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟkum vāḻvaḷitta koṟkai muttuk koḻitta poṟpuṟum puṉita nakaramākum. iṅkētāṉ ayalnāṭṭiliruntu kōṭi kōṭiyākap poṟkācukaḷum poṟpāḷaṅkaḷum vantu kuvintaṉa. koṟkai poṉkoḻikkum poṟpuṟum puṉitamāṉa paṭṭiṉam ākum.
iṅkup pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ, vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyeṉa, talaimuṟaiyākac celvap perukkuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantaṉar. koṟkai maṉṉarkaḷiṉ māḷikai aṟputamāṉa aḻakoḷirum viṇmuṭṭum māṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkiyatu. iṅkup pala araṇmaṉai kaḷum, upparikaikaḷum uyyāṉavaṉaṅkaḷum kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅ kaḷum kaṭaṟpaṭaiyum, taraippaṭaiyum, tērppaṭaiyum, parippaṭai yum, karippaṭaiyum, viṟpaṭaiyum, vēlpaṭaiyum, iruntu vantaṉa.
koṟkaiyil paḻampāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ āṭci ki.mu.500 mutal ki.pi. 300 varai iruntirukkalām eṉṟu varalāṟṟup pulavar cilar varaintuḷḷārkaḷ. appāl talainakar maturaikku māṟṟap paṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl nuṇṇaṟivu vāynta varalāṟṟu āyvu mikka aṟiñarkaḷ ki.mu.3 allatu 4 āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭilē pāṇṭiyar kōnakaram maturaikku māṟṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.1 maturaiyil talainakaram kaṭṭap paṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum poḻutu pāṇṭiya maṉṉar maturai arukil uḷḷa maṇalūr allatu kaliyāṇapuram eṉṉum ūril iruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. vaṭapāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa maturai kōnakarākac ciṟappup peṟṟeḻuntālum teṉ pāṇṭi nāṭum koṟkaiyum pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkup puṉita pūmiyāka iruntuvantaṉa. koṟkai iraṇṭāvatu talainakarāka nīṇṭa kālam perumaip paṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. maṉṉar maturaiyilum maṉṉaṉiṉ tampiyō, makaṉō iḷavaracaṉāy koṟkaik kōmāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭat tuṭaṉ koṟkaiyilum iruntu varuvāṉ. iḷavaracaṉ koṟkaiyil iruntu kaṭal vaṇikattaik kavaṉittu varuvāṉ.
koṟkaip paṭṭiṉattil iṭaic caṅkam nilaviya kālattil - atāvatu talainakar nāṉmāṭak kūṭalukku māṟṟappaṭumuṉ vāḻnta koṟkai maṉṉarkaḷ aimpattoṉpatu pēr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; eḻupattoṉpatu pēr eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷil iṟuti yil vāḻnta pāṇṭiyaṉ muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. koṟkaik kōnakaram eṉṟa peyar māṟṟappaṭṭu, kūṭalukku - (maturai) kōnakaram eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatum koṟkaippaṭṭiṉam ciṟappiḻantatu. āṉāl, aracaṉ kūṭalukkup pōyviṭṭālum iḷavaracaṉ koṟkaiyilē iruntu pāṇṭiya aracukkut tuṇai yāka iruntu vantāṉ. iḷavaracarkaḷ, koṟkaik kōmāṉ, koṟkai iḷavaracar eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ aracaṉ iṟantāl iṅkiruntu maturai pōy aracu kaṭṭilil ēṟi maṇimuṭiyum ceṅkōlum tāṅkuvar. koṟkaiyāḷi, koṟkai vēntaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭamum cūṭik koḷvar.
āṉāl, koṟkai kaṭaṟkaraip paṭṭiṉamāy iruntatu. kaṭal koṟkaiyiṉiṉṟu nāṉku kal tolaiviṟkup piṉtaḷḷip pōkavē, koṟkaiyil, kappalkaḷ vantu niṟkum vacatiyai kuṉṟiyatu ataṉāl tuṟaimukam eṉṉum peyarai iḻakka nēriṭṭatu tuṟai mukam paḻaiya kāyalukku māṟṟappeṟṟatu. koṟkai, eñciyirunta oru ciṟu perumaiyaiyum iḻantatu. maturaiyil pāṇṭiyap pēraracu vīḻntatum pāṇṭiya iḷavaracarkaḷ teṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟkut tirumpiṉar. koṟkaiyilum, tirunelvēliyilum teṉkāciyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum iruntu kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷāka vāḻntu vantaṉar. ki.pi. 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil pāṇṭiyap pēraracu maturaiyil nilaikulaintatu. āṉai iruntu aracāṇṭa iṭattil pūṉai iruntu pulampi aḻutatu pōla muculimkaḷum, nāyakkarkaḷum āṅkilēyarkaḷum māṟi māṟi āṭciyaik kaippaṟṟi makkaḷai aṭimaikaḷākki curaṇṭi vāḻntu vantaṉar.
māṟavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya makkaḷ aracuk kaṭṭilukkākac caṇṭaiyiṭṭaṉar. tilli cultāṉ utaviyaip pāṇṭiyaṉ makaṉ oruvaṉ nāṭiṉāṉ. cultāṉiṉ paṭaivīraṉ mālikkapūr perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ vantu nāṭṭaic cūṟaiyāṭip pāṇṭiya araca kumārarkaḷai aṭittu viraṭṭi nāṭṭiluḷḷa poṉṉaiyum maṇiyaiyum koḷḷaiyaṭittu ṭilli cultāṉukku aṉuppiviṭṭu aracu kaṭṭilil ēṟikkoṇṭāṉ. ki.pi. 1330 mutal 1378 varai maturaiyil muculim āṭci eḻuntatu. appāl muculim āṭciyāḷarkaḷai nāyakkarkaḷ viraṭṭi viṭṭu aracukaṭṭilaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. iṟutiyāka āṅkila aracu āṭcipīṭat til amarntatu 1947il intiyā viṭutalai peṟṟatu; pāṇṭiya nāṭu - iṉṟu intiya āṭcikkuḷ iruntuvarukiṟatu.
ki.pi. patiṉāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ piṟpakuti varai teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil pāṇṭiya araca kulattil uḷḷa cilar ciṟṟaracarkaḷ pōl āṅkāṅku āṭcipurintu vantuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ nellai teṉkāci. karivalamvantanallūr, koṟkai mutaliya ūrkaḷil iruntu koṇṭu āṭci naṭatti vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ koṟkaip pāṇṭiyaṉ. tirunelvēlip pāṇṭiyaṉ, teṉkācip pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭṭu vantaṉar. tiruccentūr māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cāttaṉkuḷattilkūṭa oru pāṇṭiyaṉ iruntu vantatākak kūṟappaṭuvatu uṇṭu.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracu nilaikulaintu uṭaintu citaṟuṇṭu pōyiṟṟu. pāṇṭiya iḷavaracarkaḷ koṟkaiyait talaimaip patiyāka vaittukkoṇṭu cila ciṟṟūrkaḷil cila iḷavaracarkaḷ iruntu āṭci naṭatti vantaṉar.1 ivarkaḷ nāyakka maṉṉarkaḷukkut tiṟai celutti vantaṉar. ciṟu pāḷaiyakkārarkaḷ pōl vāḻntu vāḻkkai naṭattiṉar. eṉṟālum mīṇṭum citaṟuṇṭa pāṇṭiyap pēraracaik kaṭṭi uyartti viṭalām eṉṟu kaṉavu kaṇṭaṉar. cilar, ataṟkāka vaiṇava camayattait taḻuvit tirumālai vēṇṭi niṉṟaṉar. cilar pārppaṉak kurumārkaḷiṉ coṟpaṭi yākam iyaṟṟi cōmāciyār eṉṉum paṭṭam tāṅki kaiyil irunta kācukaḷaiyum pārppaṉarkaḷukkup pātakāṇikkai vaittu vaṇaṅkiyum payaṉiṉṟi vaṟumaiyāl vāṭi maṟaintaṉar. cilar maṉaivi makkaḷ aṇintuḷḷa nakaikaḷai viṟṟuk kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi āṭciyaip peṟa aḻutu toḻutu naṭaip piṇaṅkaḷāyt tirintalaintu maṟaintaṉar iṟutiyāka pāṇṭiyar kuṭumpam peyar colla nātiyaṟṟup pōy viṭṭatu. iṉṟu ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷuḷ nāṉ eṉṟu koṭi vaḻippaṭṭiyal kāṭṭi urimai koṇṭāṭa evarum varakvaṭiya nilaiyillai. eṉavē inta nāṭṭil vāḻum ellā iṉattavarkaḷum tāṅkaḷē pāṇṭiya kuṭumpattiṉar eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum nilaimaiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. itu mika veṭkak kēṭākum.
caṅkak kāla ātip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ palar aṟa neṟipaṟṟi yoḻukum āṉṟa neṟiyuṭaiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. piṉṉar tōṉṟiya palar aṟaneṟi vaḻuvi maṟaneṟi taḻuvi vantuḷḷaṉar. uṟṟāriṭamum māṟṟāriṭamum pōril īvu irakkamiṉṟi naṭantu vantaṉar. caṅkak kālap pāṇṭiyaruḷ mikap pukaḻpeṟṟa palyāka cālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉpōṉ pōril puliyeṉa niṟpavaṉ. pulipōl pakaivarkaḷ mītu pāyum maṟakkulap pāṇṭiyaṉ pakaivarkaḷaik kaṇṭatum mārtaṭṭi, cavāl viṭṭu cimmakkural eḻuppip paṭaikkaḷam pukuvāṉ. avaṉatu pulikkural kēṭṭup pakaivarkaḷ kilikoṇṭu vīḻvar. avaṉ noṭip poḻutil pakaivarkaḷ mītu pāyntu veṭṭi vīḻttip puṟaṅkāṭṭiyōṭum paṭi ceytuvarum valimai vāyntavaṉ. avaṉ muṉvaitta kālai, piṉvaiyāta vīramaṟavaṉ; tōlviyē aṟiyāta tolkuṭit tōṉṟal;  veṟṟi muracoliyait tavira vēṟu oliyai avaṉ cevi maṭuttatē illai.
ivaṉ pakaivarkaḷiṭam oru ciṟitum irakkaṅkāṭṭātavaṉ. pōril pakaivarkaḷ tōlviyuṟṟāl avaṉ pakai aracaṉiṉ maṉaivi makkaḷaic ciṟaippiṭittu varuvāṉ. pakaivaṉ talaiyil muṭicūṭac ceytu taṉmuṉ maṇṭiyiṭṭu amarac ceytu taṉ kālāl avaṉ maṇi muṭiyai iṭaṟu vāṉ. tōlviyuṟṟa maṉṉaṉ talaiyai moṭṭai yaṭittu avaṉ talaimītu, cuṟṟilum cāṇiyai vaittu naṭuvil eṇṇey ūṟṟi, tiriyiṭṭu viḷakkēṟṟi aracaṉai naṭaiviḷakkākkit taṉ araṇmaṉai vāyilil niṟkac ceyvāṉ. avaṉ araṇmaṉaiyai iṭittu vīḻtti, kaḻutai pūṭṭiya ēriṉāl uḻutu karukkum kurukkum erukkum vitaippāṉ. ikkoṭuñ ceyal piṟakālat tamiḻ aracar cilariṭamum iruntatu. itu tamiḻakat tiṟkē oru cāpakkēṭāka muṭintatu!
koṟkaiyil irunta iḷavaracarkaḷē, maturaiyil maṉṉarkaḷ kālamāṉatum araca kaṭṭilai aṭaipavarkaḷāka iruppar. cilappatikāram kūṟum neṭuñceḻiyaṉ iṟantatum avaṉ tampi koṟkaiyāḷi eṉṉum paṭṭan tāṅkiya iḷavaracaṉ veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ maturai ceṉṟu maṇi muṭi cūṭi ceṅkōl ēnti, aracu kaṭṭilil amarntāṉ.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracu uṭaintapiṉ teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻntu vanta pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṟ cilaratu peyarkaḷum avarkaḷ vāḻnta kālaṅkaḷum namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai aṭiyil varumāṟu :
1. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ I 	ki.pi. 1387
2. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ II 	ki.pi. 1384-1415
3. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ III 	ki.pi. 1401-1435
4. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1393
5. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ vikkiramapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1401-1422
6. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1422-1463
7. 	arikēcari parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1422-1461
8. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1429-1473
9. 	aḻakaṉperumāḷ parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1473-1506
10. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ cīvallapaṉ 	1534-1543
11. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ parākkiramakulacēkaraṉ 	1543-1552
12. 	nelvēli māṟaṉ 	1552-1564
13. 	caṭaiyavarmaṉ ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1564-1604
14. 	varatuṅkarāma pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1588
15. 	varakuṇarāma kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 
	(kulacēkara cōmāciyār) 	1613
16.	varakuṇarāma pāṇṭiya 
	kulacēkaratēva tīṭcitar 	1748
pāṇṭiya marapiṉarkaḷiṉ āṭci ki.pi. 1748 ām āṇṭu varai talaitūkki vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iyēcu capaiyaic cērnta kattōlikkak kurumārkaḷiṉ kuṟippuppaṭi pāṇṭiyar āṭci ki.pi. 1523 mutal 1666 varai nilavip paṭippaṭiyāy aḻintu maṟaintatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. ki.pi. 1756-l kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi maturai, tirunelvēlic cīmaikaḷaik kaippaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. āṅkāṅkē cila kulatturōkikaḷai aḻaittu maṉṉar cākirtār, camīṉtār eṉṟum paṭṭam aḷittu makkaḷai āṭciyāḷarkaḷukku aṭimaippaṭutti, vari vacūl ceytu, vēṇṭiyavaikaḷaiyellām eṭuttukkoṇṭu taṅkaḷukkum ciṟitu koṭukkumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytu koṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ camīṉtārkaḷ evarāyirup piṉum avarkaḷ aṉaivarum inta nāṭṭai aṭimaippaṭutti innāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ varippaṇattil vāḻntuvanta pakaivarkaḷiṉ kaiyāṭkaḷē eṉpatai nāṭu eṉṟum maṟappataṟkillai.
koṟkai, paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷ vāḻnta mūtūr, iṭaiccaṅka mum eṇṇaṟṟa pulavarkaḷum kūṭi tamiḻāynta nallūr. pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉ paṇpumikka iṇaik kayalkaḷ poṟitta ceṅkoṭi paṟanta pērūr. kirēkkarkaḷum yavaṉarkaḷum urōmarkaḷum arāpiyarkaḷum piṟarum maṉam uvantu vantu kuṭiyēṟiya kaṭinakar. ulakil uḷḷa palvēṟu nāṭṭu marakkalaṉkaḷum namnāṭṭu nāvāy kaḷum ampikaḷum vaṅkaṅkaḷum vaṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum pōkku varattiṉ poruṭṭum naṅkūram pāyccit tattam koṭi paṟakka niṉṟa māperum paṭṭiṉam, tamiḻakattiṉ ceyporuḷkaḷaiyum viḷai poruḷkaḷaiyum ciṟappāka muttukkaḷaiyum piṟa nāṭṭiṟku viṟṟuk kōṭi kōṭiyākap poṟkācukaḷaiyum, taṅkap pāḷaṅkaḷaiyum peṟṟut tamiḻakattaip poṟkaḷañciyamākkiya mānakar.
iṉṟu anta mānakar - koṟkai, cīr aḻintu ciṟappaḻintu oru ciṟu ūrāka, paṭṭikkāṭāka tēṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭakkiṟatu. ataṉ perumaiyai mīṇṭum uyarttuvatu tamiḻarkkum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum ciṟapput taruvatākum. eṉavē tamiḻmakkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntu koṟkaiyai maṟumalarcci peṟac ceyyavēṇṭum. aṅku oru kallūri toṭaṅka vēṇṭum. toḻiṟcālai niṟuvavēṇṭum pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷaip perukkavēṇṭum. putaiporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar, atika akkaraiyeṭuttu akaḻ āyvu naṭatta vēṇṭum. tūttukkuṭit tuṟaimukam koṟkait tuṟaimukam eṉappeyar cūṭṭappeṟa vēṇṭum. muṭiyāviṭil oru mīṉ piṭittuṟaimukamākavāvatu viḷaṅkavēṇṭum. koṟkaippaṭṭiṉam. mīṇṭum nalla nākarikamāṉa nakaramāka maṟumalarcci peṟa aracāṅkam āvaṉaceyya vēṇṭum. itaṟku namatu māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellāc caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum amaiccarum tuṇai puriya vēṇṭum. aṅku oru cuṟṟulā māḷikaiyai eḻuppalām. tolporuḷ kāṭci cālaiyai niṟuvalām.

14. paḻaiya kāyalum pāṅkum
cīrvaikuṇṭam vaṭṭattil uḷḷa cāyarpurattiṟkuk kīḻpuṟam mūṉṟu kal tolaivilum tūttukkuṭi - tiruccentūr neṭuñ cālaiyil - kaṭaṟkaraiyil paḻaiya kāyal eṉṟoru ciṟṟūr iṉṟum uḷḷatu. ivvūril iṉṟum vāḻum makkaḷiṟ perum pālōr parata kulattavarē yāvar.
patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ pukaḻ peṟṟa koṟkait tuṟaimukam cīrkeṭṭup pōyiṟṟu. eṉavē paḻaiyakāyal kaṭaṟ pakutiyaip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ tuṟaimuka mākap payaṉpaṭutta muṟpaṭṭaṉar. paḻaiyakāyal pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamāy ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu kūṟa eṇṇaṟṟa nalla cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅku akaḻntu pārkkum poḻutu arēpiyar, cīṉar, urōmar nāṭṭuk kācukaḷum kōppaikaḷum pīṅkāṉkaḷ, matuppuṭṭikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. ciṟanta intiya varalāṟṟup pērāciriyar viṉceṉṟu cimītu, kāyalaip paṟṟi, tam varalāṟṟu nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
koṟkaiyil uḷḷa vaṇikat toṭarpuṭaiya ceyalkaḷ aṉaittum āṟṟiṟkuk kīḻpuṟam - mūṉṟu kaltolaivil uḷḷa kāyalil putitāka niṟuvap peṟṟa tuṟaimukattiṟku māṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. kāyal pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ varai pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟṟuṟai yākavum pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭāvatu talainakaramākavum kīḻnāṭṭu māperum vaṇikat talaimaip patikaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum naṉṟākavum viḷaṅki vantatu. patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil mārkkōpōlō eṉum ittāliya vaṇikar iṅkē tāṉ kappal ēṟiṉār. avar iṅku oru muṟai aṉṟip pala muṟai vantu pōyirukkiṟār. muṭi maṉṉarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, kuṭimakkaḷum eytap peṟṟa celvap ciṟappaiyum puṟakkōla mēṉmaiyaiyum vanta vaḻippōkkarāṉa avar kaṇṭukaḷittār; viyantu pārāṭṭi ṉār. atait tam nāḷ kuṟippil eḻutiyum vaittār. koṟkaip paṭṭiṉam pāḻpaṭak kāraṇamāka irunta atē nikaḻcciyē kāyal tuṟaimukamum kaiviṭap paṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka cīrkeṭṭu amaintatu.1
aṅkumīṉ piṭittu vāḻum cila paratakula mīṉavarkaḷum oru cila muculimkaḷum kiṟittava camayan taḻuviya cilarum oṉṟiraṇṭu intukkaḷum cila kuṭicaikaḷum eñciyuḷḷaṉa.
paḻaiya kāyal
paḻaiya kāyal, kāyal eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭum. koṟkaiyaip pōl kāyalum māṭamāḷikaikaḷ niṟainta māperum tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamāy nilaviyatu. āṉāl kāyalukku ipperumai nīṇṭa kālam nilaittirukkavillai. cila āṇṭukaḷil tuṟaimukam tūrntu pōyviṭṭatu. iṅkiruntu koṟkaiyaip pōl muttum miḷakum paṭṭum naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
paṭṭu yāṇṭiruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟum atu evvāṟu ceyyappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum urōmarkaḷ aṟiyārkaḷ. atu oru vakaip pañcu eṉṟum ētō oru cila maraṅkaḷil paṭaippumuṟai tōṟṟuvitta ōr iḷañciṟaku eṉṟum nīṇṭakālam eṇṇi vantaṉar. koṟkaiyil muttum pavaḷamum eḷitākak kiṭaittaṉa. muttu vaṇikam iṉṟaikkum mutaṉmai peṟṟē viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kīḻnāṭṭiṉarum mēṉāṭṭiṉarum muttukkuḷitta matippē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. oru muttu maṭṭum rūpāy mūvāyiram vilaipōtal aritāka nikaḻvataṉṟu - eṉṟu aṟiñar tōrmalē kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
koṟkaiyaip pōṉṟu kāyalum muttukkaḷai ēṟṟumati ceytu pukaḻ īṭṭivantatu.
mārkkō pōlō eṉṉum ittāliya nāṭṭu vaḻip pōkkar intiyā pōnta poḻutu atāvatu ki.pi. 1295-ām āṇ ṭilē koṟkait tuṟaimukam tūrntu kāyal tuṟaimukam eḻuntu viṭṭatu. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ putiya tuṟaimukamākiya kāyalilē mārkkōpōlō marakkalam ēṟiṉār eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. avar tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil taṇporunai āṟṟu mukattiliruntu oṉṟaraik kal tolaivil uḷḷa paḻaiya kāyalpaṭṭiṉam māperum vaṇikac ciṟappu vāynta tākavum nirvākat tuṟaikku ēṟṟa iṭamākavum miḷirkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum, itu pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ perum kaṭal vāṇikattiṟku oru maiyamākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē pāṇṭiyarkaḷ innakaraip pēṇi vaḷarttup pukaḻpeṟac ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṉṉum avar mēṟku nāṭukaḷiliruntu periya kalaṅkaḷ ellām kāyal tuṟaimukattai aṇukāmal pōvatillai eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. atōṭu avaratu kālattil uḷḷa pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyar vācapu ali (Wassaf Ali) intiyā, cīṉā nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉaittum kāyalukku vantu aṅkiruntu mēlai nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup paḻaiya kāyal eṉṉum pāṅkāṉa tuṟaimukam intiya nāṭṭiṟkut tiṟavu kōl pōṉṟa iṭattil amaintiruntatu. ittuṟaimukatti liruntē cēra nāṭṭu miḷakum, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muttum, karunāṭaka nāṭṭuc cantaṉak kaṭṭaikaḷum, piṟanāṭṭut tantam, kōtumai, avuri, karuvāppaṭṭai pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracar paḻaiya kāyalil uḷḷa araṇmaṉaiyil amarntiruntār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaiya kāyal pakkatti luḷḷa kaṭalil vilaiyuyarnta naṉ muttukkaḷ atikam kiṭaittaṉa. mēlum 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiliruntu kāyal tuṟai mukattil yavaṉartam eṇṇaṟṟa marakkalaṅkaḷum, cōṉakarkaḷiṉ nāvāykaḷum vantu kuḻumi niṉṟaṉa. perumaḷavil aṅku vāṇikam naṭaipeṟṟatu.
paḻaiya kāyalil, taṇ porunai, kaṭalōṭu kalakku miṭattil oru kōyil uḷḷatu. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ porunai kaṭalōṭu caṅkamam ākum iṭattil kuḷittu viṭṭu arukil uḷḷa caṅkumuka īcuvarar kōyilil ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. ik kōyilil paḻaiya kalveṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
araṇmaṉai
paḻaiya kāyal paṭṭiṉattukkuk kiḻakkē pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ vāḻnta araṇmaṉaikaḷum, kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷum iruntu aḻintu pōyiṉa. iṉṟu ataṉ aṭittaḷaṅkaḷum iṭipāṭukaḷumē kāṇakkiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
veḷināṭṭup paṇṭaṅkaḷ
cīṉa nāṭṭu uṭainta pīṅkāṉ taṭṭukaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, paḻaiya kācukaḷum iṅku, iṉṟum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku āyirak kaṇakkāṉa  kutiraikaḷ veḷināṭukaḷiliruntu kappal mūlam iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivvūril 5000 - kku mēṟpaṭṭa uṟai kiṇaṟukaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum avaikaḷaik kutiraikaḷ kuḷip pāṭṭavum, kuṭinīreṭukkavum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. iṉṟu inta uṟaikkiṇaṟukaḷ cilavaṟṟait tōṇṭit tūreṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku, akaḻntu pārkkum poḻutu eṇṇaṟṟa maṉitarkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. ivai iṅku naṭaipeṟṟa pōril uyirnītta vīrarkaḷiṉ elumpu kaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaiya kāyaliṉ vaṭapakkamuḷḷa irāmaccantira purattil perum maram eṉṟu oru marattai aḻaikkiṟārkaḷ. immarattai namatu nāṭṭil vēṟu eṅkum kāṇamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟalām. itu ātirēliyāvil uḷḷa maram eṉṟu aṟiñar kālṭuvel kūṟukiṟār. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuḷ varum vaḻakku kaḷait tīrkka im marattaṭiyil ceṉṟu cattiyam ceyvārkaḷām. āṉāl iṉṟu appaṭi etuvum naṭappatillai.
kaṭṭapommaṉ ciṉṉam
kaṭṭapommaṉ kālattuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cilavum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kaṭṭapommaṉ kaṭṭiya piḷḷaiyār kōvilum, ataṉmuṉ oru nakarā maṇṭapamum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itupōl tirucentūriliruntu pāñcālaṅkuṟicci varai pala nakarāmaṇṭapaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavām. (nakarā - muracu) tiruccentūr murukaṉ kōvilil naṭuppakal pūcaiyiṉpōtu aṭikkappaṭum nakarā aṭuttaṭuttuḷḷa nakarā maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ paṇiyāḷarkaḷ mūlam toṭarntu aṭikkappaṭṭu, pāñcālaṅ kuṟicci varai nakarā oli eṭṭum, in nakarā oliyaik kēṭṭatum kaṭṭapommaṉ pāñ cālaṅkuṟicciyil uḷḷa murukaṉ tiruuruvai vaḻipaṭṭup piṉ uṇavu uṇpāṉām!
kāyalil kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kōṭṭaiyai nirmāṇittavar maṉṉar cūrapatma rājā eṉṟum, ivarē iṟutiyāka vāḻnta pāṇṭiya aracar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ippeyar nāyakka maṉṉar peyar pōlat terikiṟatu. cilar itu mukamatiya maṉṉar peyar eṉ kiṉṟaṉar. itu tavaṟu. kāyalil uḷḷa mīṉāṭci kōvilil cūrrācā viṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭa oru ceppēṭu uḷḷatu. atu iṉṉum paṭikkappaṭavillai. atu akkōvil pūcāriyiṭam uḷḷatu. atu paṭikkappaṭṭāl cila uṇmaikaḷ veḷippaṭalām. iṅkuḷḷa cūraṅkāṭu eṉṉum ciṟṟūr cūr aracaṉ peyarāl tōṉṟiya ūr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kutirai vaṇikam ceyyavanta arāpiyar ivvūrukkarukē kuṭiyēṟiṉar. avvūrē iṉṟu kāyalpaṭṭiṉam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvūril iṉṟuḷḷa makkaḷiṟ perumpālōr arapikaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉar eṉṟē kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum marakkalaṅ kaḷil vantatāl marakkalarāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ic collē maraikkāyar eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. kīḻakkarai, nākappaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa kaṭaṟkaraikaḷil vāḻum muculīmkaḷum maraikkāyar eṉṟē aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
mārkkōpōlō
aṟiñar iyūl (Colonel yule), tam, mārkkōpōlō eṉṉum nūlil kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu kāyal eṉṉum pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟaiyum paṭṭiṉamumākiya iṭam aḻivuṟṟup pal lāṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē aṟiñarkaḷāl kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. kāyal ceṉṉai mānilattil, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kaṭaṟkaraiyil nīṇṭakālamākap pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ tuṟaimuka māka viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpatu makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉiṉṟum maṟantu pōyviṭṭatu. intap paṭṭiṉam paṟṟi ki.pi. 1292-l mārkkō pōlōvāl viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. naṭu ūḻiyil intiyāviṉ kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkaraiyil oru kaṭaṟṟuṟaiyākavum mika mukkiyamāṉa nakaramāyum viḷaṅkiya ivvūr potuvāka aṇṭai yil vāḻum makkaḷāl paḻaiya kāyal eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭu vantatu. itu tāmiravaruṇi āṟṟiṟku aṇmaiyil, ataṉ āṟṟu mukattiṟku oṉṟaraik kaltolaivil iruntatu eṉṟu nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tirunelvēli varalāṟṟai varainta aṟiñar muṉaivar irāparṭṭu kālṭuvel avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1
mārkkōpōlō iṅku vantiruntapōtu kāyalil kaṇṭavai avaratu kavaṉattai īrttaṉa. kāyal perum paṭṭiṉamāka vum ciṟanta nakaramākavum maṉṉariṉ aintu kumārarkaḷil mūtta makaṉāṉa acar (Ashar) eṉpavarukku urittāṉatākavum iruntatu. ōrṉacu, kicu (pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāvil uḷḷa oru tīvu) ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta kappalkaḷellām intat tuṟaimukattil tam naṅkūrattaip pāycci niṉṟukoṇṭiruntaṉa. aṉṟiyum aṭīy (Adeei), arēpiyā, ilēṭaṉ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉṟu vanta marakkalaṅkaḷ viṟpaṉaikkākat tam nāṭṭuk kutiraikaḷaiyum piṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum koṇṭu vantu iṟakkik koṇṭiruntaṉa. itu, anta ūrilum ataic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa makkaḷai oṉṟu tiraḷumpaṭi ceykiṟatu. eṉavē kāyal eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattil perum vaṇikam naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu. aracariṭam ērāḷamāṉa celvam kuvintu kiṭantatu. aracaṉ vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ iḻaikkappeṟṟa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷait taṉ kātilum, kaḻut tilum, kaikaḷilum, iṭuppilum aṇintiruntāṉ. avaṉ māperum aracaik kavaṉamākak kāttu naṭatti varukiṟāṉ. avaṉatu āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa nāṭu naṉku iyaṅki varukiṟatu aṅku nītiyum, nērmaiyum, oppuravup paṇpum uṇṭu. vaṇikarkaḷukkum, ayal nāṭṭiṉarkkum nalla ātaravum, aṉpum, calukaiyum aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivaṉuṭaiya nakaraip pārppataṟku nāṉ mikavum makiḻcci aṭaikiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mārkkōpōlō kālattil intiyā pōnta pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyar vācapu māpāriṉ tuṟaimukam kāyal eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟi māpāraip paṟṟik kūṟiyirukkiṟār. ciṉ, mecciṉ eṉṉum ariya nakaraṅkaḷiliruntu (cīṉāvil vaṭakkut, teṟkup pakutikaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷ) vanta malai pōṉṟa caṅ eṉṟa kappalkaḷ intu, cintu, lēṭaṉ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu aḻakiya poruṭkaḷai ēṟṟikkoṇṭu kāṟṟiṉ iṟakkaikaḷaik koṇṭu nīriṉmītu paṟantu vantu āṅku eppoḻutum varuvatum, pōvatu māka irukkiṉṟaṉa. pāracīka vaḷai kuṭāviṉ celvaṅkaḷ, kuṟippāka īrākku, kurōcāṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu, ēṉaiya nāṭukaḷai aṇi ceyyum poruṭkaḷ, māpāriliruntu airōppāviṟkum urōmiṟkum celvataṟku vaḻiyāka paḻaiya kāyal intiyāviṉ tiṟavukōlāka irukkiṟatu1 eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ayalār ātikkam
14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkak kālattilē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku ēḻarai nāṭṭuc caṉiyaṉ piṭittuviṭṭatu. mūṉṟu tamiḻ maṉṉar kaḷum nilai kulaiya muṟpaṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. 1327il ṭilli aracaṉ mukammatu piṉ tukḷak māpār nāṭṭait taṉ kālaṭiyil koṇṭu vantāṉ. ki.pi. 1335il calāl uttīṉ acaṉ eṉpavaṉai araca paripālaṉāka niyamittāṉ. āṉāl avaṉ maturaiyil taṉiyāṭci niṟuvikkoṇṭāṉ. 1340 kiyācu uttīṉ maturai maṉṉaṉākit tamiḻakam muḻuvataiyum accuṟuttiṉāṉ. 1378 - varai muculim āṭci naṭai peṟṟatu.
appāl vicayanakara nāyakkarkaḷ, muculim āṭciyai akaṟṟit taṅkaḷ āṭciyai niṟuviṉar. 16ām, nūṟṟāṇṭil vicuvanāta nāyakkaṉ (1529-64) āṭci tirucciyiliruntu kumarimuṉai varaip paraviyatu. 1565-ām āṇṭil maturaiyil tirumalai nāyakkaṉ āṭci ceyta kālattil iyēcu capaip pātiri mārkaḷ maturaiyil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ki.pi.1675il makārāṭṭirar āṭci taḷirttatu. civāciyiṉ tampi veṅkāci pōṉcalē 1676il tañcaiyil nāyakkar āṭciyai oḻittu aracu kaṭṭilēṟiṉāṉ. 1498il pōrccukkīciya mālumivācu kōṭakāmā marakkalattiṉ mūlam āppirikkāvaic cuṟṟivantu kōḻikkōṭṭai (kaḷḷik kōṭṭai)yil kāl vaittāṉ. akkālattē paḻaiya kāyalil muttuk kuḷikkum toḻil paratavarkaḷiṭamiruntu muculimkaḷiṭam pōyviṭṭatu. ki.pi. 1514il ittuṟaimukattiṟku pōrccukkīciyak kappaliṉ kappittāṉ (Captain) pār pōcā varumpoḻutu kāyaliṉ muttuk kuḷikkum toḻil kollattil uḷḷa oru aracaṉukku uriyatāy iruntatu. 1542ām āṇṭu tūya cavēriyār muttuk kuḷikkum tuṟaiyai mutaṉmutalākap pārvaiyiṭa vantār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. appoḻutu paṭṭiṉam muṟṟilum pōrccukkīciyar kaikkup pōyviṭṭatu.
paḻaiya kāyaliṉ pakkattē kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, mañcaḷ nīrk kāyal, puṉṉaikkāyal vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ paṭṭiṉam māṟaṉmaṅkalam eṉṟu pala ūrkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉṉaik kāyal akkālattil paratavarkaḷukku oru nalla kuṭiyēṟṟap pakutiyāy iruntatu. 1552-ām āṇṭil puṉṉaikkāyal oru ciṟu kaṭaṟṟuṟaiyākak kūṭa iruntatu. iṅku oru maṇṇālāṉa kōṭṭaiyum eḻuppap peṟ ṟiruntatu. akkōṭṭai pōrccukkīciyak kappittāṉ kōvārṭ ṭiṉō ātikkattiṉkīḻ iruntatu. ivaṉ paṭakarkaḷāl tōṟkaṭik kappaṭṭāṉ, eṉṟālum pōrccukkīciyar paṭai tiraṭṭi vantu mīṇṭum kōṭṭaiyaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.
mutal accakam
puṉṉaik kāyalil pōrccukkīciyar taṅkaḷ araciyal ātikkattai nilaināṭṭa camayattai nilaināṭṭi ataṉ mūlam araciyal ātikkattai uṟutippaṭuttat tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar puṉṉaik kāyalil oru accakattai niṟuvi atil tuṇṭut tāḷkaḷ acciṭṭu vantaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷukku kiṟittava camayattaip pōtikka oru tamiḻ nūlai veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. annūl puṉṉaik kāyal accakattil 1578ām āṇṭu, aṭiyār varalāṟu (Flos Sanctrum) eṉṟa peyarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭa nūṟkaḷil itu mūṉṟāvatu nūl eṉṟeṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ maṟupatippu ip poḻutu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai tūya cavēriyār kallūrit tamiḻt tuṟait talaivar tavattiru. muṉaivar irācamāṇikkam aṭika ḷāriṉ perum muyaṟciyāl tūttukkuṭi tamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻaka veḷiyīṭāka veḷivantuḷḷatu.1 iḵtaṉṟi irumaruntakamum, urōmaṉ kattōlikkac camaya pōtaṉaik kūṭamum (Seminary) kōṭṭaik kāvaṟ paṭaiyum iruntaṉa. 1596-ām āṇṭu kāyalil mīṉ piṭikkum tuṟaiyiṉ taḷapatiyāy oruvar paḻaiya kāyalil iruntu vantār.
iṭoṉ cepattiyar eṉpavar, kiṟittavap paratavarkaḷ muttukkuḷittu muttu eṭuttu vantāl atil pattil oru paṅku kiṟittavakkōyil makamaikkuk koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu oru vitiyai ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. tamiḻakattil muculim āṭci arumpiya poḻutu muculimkaḷum muttuk kuḷikkavum mīṉ piṭikkavum muṟ paṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmaiyāṉa iṉattavar kaḷāṉa paratavarkaḷukkum muculimkaḷukkum mōtalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl aṉṟaiyap pāṇṭiyanāṭṭu āṭciyāḷar muculim āka iruntatāl tamiḻnāṭṭup paratarkaḷ ātaravu iṉṟit tavittaṉar; avarkaḷ muculimkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟu vantaṉar. pāṇṭiya nāṭu pōnta pōrccukkīciyar paratavarkaḷukku ātaravu kāṭṭiyatōṭu kattōlikka camayattilum avarkaḷ iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytu viṭṭaṉar. appāl pōrccukkīciyar kattōlik paratavarkaḷukku nalla araṇ pōṉṟiruntaṉar. nāyakka aracarkaḷāl muculim āṭci tamiḻakattil maṟaiyavē, kattōlikkap paratavarkaḷ kaṭal toḻilait taṅkaḷ kālaṭiyil vaittuk koṇṭaṉar. muculimkaḷ at toḻili ṉiṉṟu peritum vilakik koṇṭaṉar. itaṉ palaṉākap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa kaṭaṟṟuṟaikaḷil vāḻnta paḻantamiḻk kuṭikaḷāṉa paratavarkaḷ aṉaivarum kattōlikka kiṟittavarkaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉar. āṉāl tañcai, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉaiyilum uḷḷa paratavarkaḷiṟ cilar intukkaḷākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. ki.pi. 1605il ulāntakkārarkaḷ, vaṇikam eṉṉum pērāl intiyā pōntaṉar. 1658il ulāntakkārarkaḷ pōrccukkīci yaraip puṉṉaik kāyaliṉiṉṟu viraṭṭi aṭittatuṭaṉ kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkaraiyiṉiṉṟum ōṭumpaṭi ceytaṉar. ulāntakkārarkaḷ maturai maṉṉaṉiṭamiruntu muttuk kuḷippu, caṅkuk kuḷippu, mīṉ piṭipput toḻilkaḷiṉ muḻu urimaiyaiyum peṟṟaṉar. 1664il intiyāviṟkup pireñcukkārarkaḷ vantaṉar.1 avarkaḷ kattōlikkap paratavarkaḷukku peritum ātaravaḷittu vantaṉar. 1795il āṅkilēyar kaikku āṭci māṟum varai tamiḻakak kaṭal ātikkam ulāntakkārarkaḷiṭamē iruntu vantatu. itaṉāl 1825il kiṟittava camayappaṇi pīṭuṟṟatu.
koṟkaiyiṉ poṟpuṟum tuṟaimukam 12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil kaṭalkōḷāl maṇmēṭiṭṭut tuṟaimukattiṟkuriya vacatiyai iḻantatu. ataṉ piṉ, paḻaiya kāyal pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ tuṟaimukamāka eḻuntatu. ïj‹ fhy« ek¡F¤ â£lt£ltkhf¡ TwKoaÉšiy v‹whY« ï¤jhÈa eh£L tÊ¥ ngh¡f® kh®¡nfhnghnyh 13M« ü‰wh©o‹ ïWâÆš - m~jhtJ 1292ïš fhaš JiwKf¤â‰F tªJ f¥gš V¿dh® v‹W bjÇ»wJ.* koṟkait tuṟaimukam kai naḻuvappaṭavirunta atē kaṭalkōḷ, paḻaiya kāyal tuṟaimukamum kainaḻuva viṭappaṭuvataṟ kum kāraṇamāyt tōṉṟiyatu. ataṉāl pōrccukkīciyar 1580-ām āṇṭil tūttukkuṭiyil kuṭiyēṟi atait tuṟaimukam ākkiṉar. puṉṉaik kāyalaiyum avarkaḷ taṅkum iṭamāka ākkik koṇṭa ṉar. 1658ām āṇṭil ulāntakkārarkaḷ (Dutch) tūttukkuṭit tuṟaimukattaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. 1782-ām āṇṭil ulāntak kārarkaḷiṭamiruntu tūttukkuṭit tuṟaimukattaik kiḻak kintiyak kampeṉiyār 1782ām āṇṭil paṟittuk koṇṭaṉar. mīṇṭum 1785ām āṇṭil ulāntakkārarkaḷ kaikkup pōy 1796ām āṇṭil kampeṉiyār āṭcikku vantatu. mīṇṭum 1818ām āṇṭu pepruvarit tiṅkaḷ 9ām nāḷ ulāntākkārarkaḷ kaikkup pōyiṟṟu. piṉṉart tūttukkuṭit tuṟaimukam 1825ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷ 1ām nāḷ amaitiyāy - camātāṉattuṭaṉ - āṅkila āṭciyiṉ kaikkup pōyiṟṟu.  iṟutiyāy 1947ām āṇṭu ākattut tiṅkaḷ 15ām nāḷ āṅkilēyar, intiyarka ḷiṭam mika amaitiyāy tūttukkuṭit tuṟaimukattaiyum - ēṉ? intiyāvaiyum intiyarkaḷiṭam oppuvittuviṭṭu iṅkilān tiṟkuk kappal ēṟik kaṭuki akaṉṟuviṭṭaṉar.1
mārkkōpōlō tamatu nūlil teṉṉintiyāvaip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu,2 māpār nāṭṭiṉ māperum mānilattil pāṇṭiyanāṭu perumai vāyntatu. iṅkuḷḷa nāṭukaḷ aṉaitti ṉum ciṟappu vāyntatu. iṅku aintu uṭaṉpiṟantārkaḷāṉa aracarkaḷ āṭci purikiṟārkaḷ. inta mānilam ulakilēyē uyarnta paṇpu vāyntatum, nērttiyāṉatumākum. itaṉai āṭcipuriyum aracarkaḷil cuntarapāṇṭiyat tēvar eṉpavar muṭicūṭiya maṉṉar āvar. avaruṭaiya āṭciyil arumperum aḻakiya muttukkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
kāyal periyatum aḻakiyatumāṉa paṭṭiṉam. ataṉuṭaiya paḻam perumaiyum niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum iṉṉum āynta kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavillai. iṉṟaiyak kāyalukku vaṭakkē mūṉṟu, nāṉku kal tolaivil māṟamaṅkalam (māṟōkkam) eṉṉum ūrukku oṉṟaraik kal aḷavil uḷḷa camaveḷiyil uṭainta pāṉai caṭṭikaḷum, paḻaṅkācukaḷum, piṟaporuṭkaḷum kiṭait tiruppatu mārkkōpōlōviṉ aṟikkaiyai uṟutippaṭuttu kiṟatu. kāyal ōr oppaṟṟa kaṭaṟṟuṟai eṉṟu muculim varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ varaivatai iṅkuk kiṭaikkum cīṉa, arāpiya nāṭṭup pīṅkāṉkaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum, poṉ, veḷḷi, ceppuk kācukaḷum uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
kāyalil vāḻum toṉmai vāynta tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭiyaic cērnta tamiḻp paratavarkaḷ ēṟattāḻa 30-āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, āṅkila āṭci nilavum kālattil kāyaliṉ paḻam perumaiyum, ariya ciṟappukkaḷumākiyavaṟṟai eṭuttukkāṭṭik kāyal tuṟaimukattai maṟumalarcci aṭaiyacceyya muyaṉṟaṉar. tūttukkuṭiyai viṭak kāyal periya tuṟaimukamāvataṟkuriya ellā vāyppaiyum peṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟu aracāṅkattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. āṉāl aṉṟu āṭcip pīṭattil amarntirunta āṅkila āṭciyāḷar ceviyil avarkaḷ vārttai aṇuvaḷavum pukavillai. kāyal makkaḷ attuṭaṉ toṭarntu kiḷarcci ceyya villai. cōrntu iruntu viṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṭam makkaḷ palamum illai; pōtiya celvamum illai.
nēru āṭciyil tūttukkuṭiyaip periya tuṟaimukamākka muṉvantaṉar. atu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatēyākum. āṉāl tūttuk kuṭit tuṟaimukam koṟkait tuṟaimukam allatu kāyal tuṟai mukam eṉṟa peyar peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. varalāṟṟu rītiyākap pārttāl koṟkait tuṟaimukam eṉṟu peyariṭap peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṭuvaṇ aracāṅkattiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭi irukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻar varalāṟṟaic cemmaiyāka aṟiyāta cilar tamiḻaka araciṉ talaimaip pīṭattil iruntatāl atu naṭaipeṟavillai!
eṉṟālum, iṉṟu, tamiḻakattil tamiḻaracu taḷirt tuḷḷatu. tamiḻ moḻiyaiyum tamiḻ varalāṟṟaiyum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭai yum pēṇa muṉvantuḷḷa iṉṟaiya tamiḻaracu itai naṭuvaṇ aracukku eṭuttuc colla vēṇṭum. itaṟkākak koṟkai makkaḷum, ciṟappāka tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa makkaḷum, potu vākat tamiḻakamum āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.
āṟṟiṉāl eḻunta pirivukaḷ
arāpi nāṭṭiliruntu vantu kuṭiyēṟa virumpiya makkaḷukkup puvic cakkaravartti ceyavīrarācu kāruvāl koṭukkappaṭṭa paṭṭayattil pala ūrkaḷ cērntutāṉ kāyal eṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. mēlum taṇporunaiyāṟu ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu pala kiḷaikaḷākap pirintataṉāl periya nakar pirintu, muḷḷakkāṭu, kulaiyaṅ karical, pullāveḷi, kōvaṅkāṭu, aṭaikkalapuram, mañcaḷ nīrkkāyal, akaram, māṟa maṅkalam, koṭuṅ kaṇi, kuruvittuṟai, cērnta pūmaṅkalam, puṉṉaikkāyal, kīṟaṉūr, āvaraṅkāṭu, canta ecuttēvu, kurucaṭi, kāyal paṭṭiṉam eṉṟa peyarkaḷāl pala ciṟṟūrkaḷāyiṉa.
nāṭṭuppāṭal
paḻaiya kāyalil peṇ makkaḷum kuḻantaikaḷum pāṭivarum nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ cila uḷa :
pāṭip pāṭit taṇṇīr eṭukkum pāṇṭiya nakarkāyal
ūraiccuṟṟip puḷiyamaram uluppi viṭṭāl kalakalakkum
pēraic cuṟṟik kūppiṭuṅkaḷ pērāṉa kāyalūrai
caṇṭai piṭikkum mukkāṇi; cāṭci collum māṟamaṅkalam
maṇṭipōṭṭu vaḻakkut tīrkkum makarācaṉ kāyalūr.
eṉap palappāṭal iṉṟum pāṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟappu
arc, cavēriyāriṉ intiyamicaṉ eṉṟa nūlil paḻamai mikka (St Francis Xaviers Indian Mission) paḻaiya kāyaliṉ ciṟappu cīrum pala uḷa.
paḻamai
pāṇṭiya aracu ki.mu. 600 - 700 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ceḻip puṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu mekacutaṉicu eṉṟu cantira kuptaṉiṉ avaiyil irunta kirēkkat tūtuvar kūṟuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.1
paṟipōṉa celvaṅkaḷ
tillic cultāṉiṉ taḷapati cēykak camāluttiṉ, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu vantu atai veṉṟu 700 erumaikaḷ cumakkattakka koḷḷaip poruḷkaḷai 1373ām āṇṭil tillikku aṉuppi vaittuḷḷāṉ. 80āṇṭu toṭarntu koḷḷai yaṭikkappaṭṭu vantatāl nāṭu avaṉāl vaṟumaik kaṭalil mūḻkiyatu eṉṟālum vicayanakara aracaṉ kālattil paḻaiya kāyal peritum paviciḻantatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.2
paḻaiya kāyal pakutikaḷukku māṟōkam, pāval, kāval, kāpil, māpar eṉṟu pala peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅki vantatāka mārkkō pōlō kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tiṟavukōl
uvāciṅ eṉṟa pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ cīṉā, cintu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu mukkiyamāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum, viḷaiporuṭ kaḷaiyum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu varum ayal nāṭṭuk kappalkaḷ kāyalukku vantuviṭṭup piṉ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāracīkam, carāka, kurācam, ram mutaliya nāṭukaḷ, muṉṉāl atika celvam peṟṟiruntataṟkuk kāraṇam intiyā viṟkut tiṟavukōl pōlirunta kāyalē eṉṟu mārkkōpō lō kūṟiṉār eṉṟu muṉaivar kālṭuvel tirunelvēli varalāṟṟil (pakkam 28) kūṟiyuḷḷār.
muttu
peyṉ nāṭṭut tol poruḷ kāṭci cālaiyil mikap parumaṉāṉa muttu oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. atu koṟkaikkum, kāyalukkum arukil uḷḷa kaṭalil eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
vācukōṭi kāmā, 1498il mutaṉ mutalāka intiyāvaik kaṇṭa airōppiyar eṉṟu cila varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. atu mikattavaṟu, ataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē pāṇṭiyarkaḷ urōmaip pēraracuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭu vaṇikam vaḷarttuḷḷaṉar. vācukōṭikāmā intiyā pōnta poḻutu kāyalil uḷḷavarkaḷ pala muttumālaikaḷaik kaḻuttil aṇintu koṇṭiruntatākak kūṟukiṉṟār. appoḻutu muttuk kuḷippatu oru muculim aracaṉ kaiyil iruntatu. 1658il muculim kaḷai ulāntak kārarkaḷ kārmaṇal eṉṟa (Coromandal Coast) muttuk kuḷittuṟaiyiṉiṉṟu viraṭṭiviṭṭu, kāyalilum piṟa iṭaṅ kaḷilum paṇṭakacālaikaḷai niṟuviṉar. avarkaḷ appoḻutu maturaiyil āṭci purinta maṉṉariṭamiruntu muttuk kuḷikkavum caṅkukaḷ eṭukkavum urimai peṟṟaṉar. mīṉ piṭikka aṉumati peṟṟatiṉāl avarkaḷukku atikamāṉa varuvāy vantatu. 1795ām āṇṭu toṭaṅki iṟutiyāy 1825ām āṇṭu varai āṅki lēyarkaḷ perum muyaṟci ceytu nāṭṭiṉ ātikkam aṉaittai yum kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.1
kāyalil, pāṇṭiyar, cōḻar, cērar, pallavar, teluṅkar, kaṉṉaṭar, cīṉar, parmiyar, arāpiyar, kirēkkar, urōmar pōṉṟa pala nāṭṭavarkaḷiṉ kācukaḷum, kōppaikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
tūya. tōmaiyār iṅkē karaiyēṟiyataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka iṅku oru ciṟu kōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu puṉṉaik kāyalukku vaṭapuṟam irukkiṟatu.
ki.mu. 302ām āṇṭu caiṉa camayattiṉar iṅku pōntaṉar. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ uyirōmpum neṟiyai makkaḷukkup pōtittu vantuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iṅku iruntaṉa. iṉṟum pala camaṇac cilaikaḷ iṅku uḷḷaṉa.
iṉṟum iṅku caṅku makēcuvarar kōyilum tirumāl kōyi lum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukku mēṟkē aḻivuṟṟuk kiṭakkum kōyilkaḷ muculimkaḷiṉ paṭaiyeṭuppāl aḻikkap paṭṭavai.
1543ām āṇṭil tūya cavēriyārāl iṅku oru kattō likkak kōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ulāntākkārarkaḷāl oru piriviṉaik kiṟittavak kōyilum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. alā vutīṉiṉ paṭaittalaivaṉ, aṭakkappaṭṭa oru kōriyum, alāvutīṉ paṇiyāṭkaḷil oruvaṉ aṭakkappaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭum 40-kiyūpik aṭinīḷa muḷḷa kallaṟaiyum maṟṟumoru muculim kōriyum kāppirik cāmi kōyilil vaṇaṅkappaṭum āppirikka nāṭṭuc cilai oṉṟum iṅku uḷḷaṉa.1

15. kāyalpaṭṭiṉam
taṇporunai āṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭattil āṟṟiṟku vaṭapāl koṟkaip paṭṭiṉam iruntatu. āṟṟiṟkut teṟkē āttūrukku aṇmaiyil - teṉpuṟam kāyalpaṭṭiṉam amain tuḷḷatu. iṉṟu ivvūr tirunelvēli - tiruccentūr iruppup pātaiyil irukkiṟatu.
9ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷukku arāpiya nāṭṭiliruntu cila arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷ kutiraikaḷai marakkalattil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu vantu koṟkai tuṟaimukattil iṟakki viṟpaṉai ceytu vantaṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷukku āṇṭutōṟum arāpi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu 10,000 kutiraikaḷ iṟakkumati yāyiṉa. mēlum tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu iñci, miḷaku, nīlam mutaliya vaṟṟai vāṅkit tam kappalil arāpi vaṇikarkaḷ ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. ivvaṇikap perukkāl pala arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷum vaṇikap piratinitikaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ paṇiyāṭkaḷum koṟkaikku arukē taṇporunai āṟṟiṉ teṉpāl kaṭaṟkaraiyil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅ kiṉar. avarkaḷ innāṭṭiṟku marakkalattil ēṟivantatāl marakkala rāyar, marakkāyar, maraikkār eṉṟellām aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻum iṭam cōṉakar paṭṭiṉam, cōṉakar cēri eṉṟum, āṟṟiṟku vaṭakkē veḷḷaiyar kuṭiyēṟiya iṭam veḷḷaiyaṉ irukkai, yavaṉaccēri eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa. cōṉakarkaḷ tam peṇkaḷai aḻaittu varavillai. taṉṉantaṉiya rāka vantu tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷaiyē maṇantu koṇṭaṉar. ākaiyāl avarkaḷiṉ tāymoḻi tamiḻāyiṟṟu.
vaṇikam vaḷara vaḷara arāpiyarkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu vantu tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. iṉṟu avarkaḷ vāḻum iṭam kāyal paṭṭiṉam, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṭam 12000 - arāpitiṉārukku vāṅkappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
arāpiyarkaḷ kutirai vaḷarppatai naṉku terintavarkaḷ; kutirai ilakkaṇamum naṉku uṇarntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ oru kutirai yiṉ vilai 100-mārkku veḷḷiyiṉ peṟumatikkuk kūṭutalāṉa 500-cakkip poṉ vilai ākum. kutiraikaḷ vāṅkuvatil pāṇṭiyarkaḷ, nāṭṭiṉ celvattiṉ perum pakutiyai vīṇākki vantaṉar. avarkaḷ vāṅkum kutiraikaḷil pala uyiriḻantu vantaṉa. avai kaḷai eppaṭi vaḷarppatu, eṉṉa tīṉi aḷippatu eṉpatu avar kaḷukkut teriyātu eṉṟellām mārkkapōlō kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl, pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkuk kutirai ilakkaṇam naṉku teriyum; kutiraikaḷai vaḷarkkavum naṉku teriyum eṉpatu tiruviḷaiyāṭal purāṇam, acuva parīṭcai mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉ mūlam naṉkaṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
kutirai viṟka vantavarkaḷ aṉṟi vēṟu pala arāpiyarkaḷum iṅku vantu kuṭiyēṟa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. 13āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil, ceyyat camāluttīṉ eṉṉum periyār talaimaiyil arāpi muculimkaḷ palar kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. tiru.ceyyatu camāluttīṉ, periyār mukammat napi avarkaḷiṉ 21-āvatu talaimuṟaiyil vantavarkaḷ. ekittil kalipā koṭuṅkōl āṭci purintār. mēlum aṅkup pūmi atircciyum veḷḷap perukkum eḻuntaṉa. eṉavē aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṟ cilar ki.pi. 1284 paḻaiya kāyal tuṟaimukattil vantu iṟaṅkik kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. appoḻutu cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ āṭci naṭaipeṟṟatu. varalāṟṟu āciriyar caṭavarmaṉ iraṇṭām cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nāṭṭai āṇṭu vantāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ āṭciyil kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil vāḻnta mukammatu tīpiyiṉ makaṉ takiyyuttīṉ aptul rakumāṉ pāṇṭiya maṉṉariṉ piratinitiyāyum ālōcakarāyum iruntār eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu. kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kiṭaittuḷḷa arāpik kalveṭṭiṉ paṭi pala muculimkaḷukku nayiṉār eṉṟa paṭṭam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tākat terikiṟatu. cēkkāli nayiṉārāṉa ceṇpaka mutaliyār akamatu nayiṉārāṉa vīrapāṇṭiya mutaliyār mutaliya paṭṭaṅ kaḷaip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ muculimkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. nayiṉār, ūriṉ nāṭṭāṇmaikkārar āvar. avar vaḻakkukaḷai vicārittut tīrppuc ceyvār. avar paḷḷi vāyilukku varum poḻutu paṇiyāḷar avarukku araca kuṭai piṭittuk koṇṭu varuvārkaḷ. paṭṭattu nayiṉārkaḷukkuc ciṟappāṉa āṭaikaḷ uṇṭu.
ki.pi. 1553il pāṇṭiya maṉṉar muculimkaḷ cilarukku aḷitta paṭṭayam iṅkiruppatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ iṟutik kālattilum appāl maturai nāyakka maṉṉar āṭciyilum iṅkuḷḷa muculimkaḷ ciṟappāṉa calukaikaḷ vantu peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.
iṅku 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa arāpi nāṇayaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku akaḻntu kaṇṭa cila poṟ kācukaḷ ceṉṉait tolporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil uḷḷaṉa.

16. ātittanallūrum aḻipāṭukaḷum
ātittanallūr paṟampu eṉpatu tirunelvēli - tiruccentūr neṭuñcālaiyil uḷḷa oru mēṭṭu nilam. itu pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyiliruntu 11-kal tol tolaivil taṇ porunai āṟṟiṉ vaṭakaraiyil uḷḷatu. ivviṭattil iṉṟu ūr etuvum illai. āṉāl makkaḷ ippakutiyiṉai āticcanallūrp paṟampu eṉṟu aḻaikkiṟārkaḷ. ippaṟampil makkaḷ vāḻntataṟkuriya aṟikuṟikaḷ etuvum kiṭaikkavillai. pakkattil periya nakaram etuvum iruntirukkalām. annakaramakkaḷ itaip putaikuḻiyāka payaṉpaṭutti irukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
mutaṉmutalāka 1876ām āṇṭu cermaṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa perliṉ nakaraiccērnta muṉaivar cākar1 eṉpavar iṅkup pōntu akaḻ āyvu naṭattiṉār. atil, pala īmattāḻikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  ivai mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ (mutumattaṉ tāḻi) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. ittāḻikaḷil pala elumpukkūṭukaḷum poṉ, irumpu pōṉṟa vaṟṟāl ceyyappeṟṟa pala poruḷkaḷum pala aḻakiya maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa. avai aṉaittum perviṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
mīṇṭum iṅkē pirañcu nāṭṭiluḷḷa pārīcu nakarattaic cārnta em. lūyi lēppikkiyū eṉṉum aṟiñarāl 1903 il akaḻ āyvu naṭattap peṟṟatu. 
mēlum pala cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa tāḻikaḷum maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷum, irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṭaikkalaṅkaḷum, taṅkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷum, pittaḷai aṇikalaṉkaḷum kaltirikaikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ittu nainta tuṇikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷum arici, varaku mutaliya kūlapporuḷkaḷiṉ umikaḷum tāḻiyiṉuḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku atikamāṉa civappu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. pittaḷaip pāttiraṅkaḷum irumpāl ceyyap peṟṟa viḷakkukaḷumākap pala poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṉai vatil vallavarkaḷāy iruntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ulōka vēlaip pāṭṭilum necavut toḻililum, taccut toḻililum kūṭa ciṟantiruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum maṇ veṭṭi, koḻu mutaliyavaikaḷiṉ mūlam, iṅku nilaviyatu, āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭu eṉṟum ivarkaḷatu mukkiya toḻil payirt toḻil eṉṟum terikiṉṟaṉa. pittaḷaiyil vilaṅkukaḷ, pūcceṭikaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷai urukki vārttiruppatu avarkaḷiṉ kaittoḻiṟ ciṟappaik kāṭṭukiṟatu. arappā makkaḷaip pōl ātittanallūr makkaḷum timil perutta kāḷaic ciṉṉamum nakara nākarikamum peṟṟa makkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
aṟiñar em. lāppikkiyū avarkaḷiṉ, 1903-1904 ām āṇṭaiya āyvil, ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷaic cārnta (Proto Dravididan race) toṭakka kālap paṇpāṭu eṉṟu muṭipu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1 cintu veḷiyai āynta car cāṇ mārcal tam cīriya nūlil ivvūraik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.2
itaṉait toṭarntu 1899 mutal 1905 varai ceṉṉai aracāṅkat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai mēṟpārvaiyāḷarum akaḻ āyvuttuṟai atikāriyumāṉa aleccāntar irī (Alexander Rea F.S.A. Scot), avarkaḷiṉ ariya muyaṟciyāl ippakutiyai akaḻntu, aṅkuk kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ, ceṉṉait tol poruḷ kāṭcicālaiyil, paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ tokutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa paḻam poruḷkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻikkuppiṉ eḻunta pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ mūlattai uṭaiyatu eṉṟu avar karutiṉār. āṉāl pērāciriyar ji. eliyaṭcimit, kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattiṟku muṉ - paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku - muṉ paḻam pāṇṭiyar kālattil ippakutikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukka lām eṉṟu karutukiṟār.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṇṭai ōṭukaḷil pala, tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭai ōṭukaḷ eṉṟum, oṉṟu āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭu eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.3 eṉavē kiṟittava ūḻi arumpu muṉṉarē tirā viṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pūmarāṅ eṉṉum - pakaivarkaḷ mītu eṟintāl, avarkaḷait tākkiviṭṭu vīciyavarkaḷ kaikku - varum oru vakai āyutam tamiḻakattil paṇṭu toṭṭuk kiṭaippatum oru cāṉṟākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
ātitta nallūr nākarikam arappā nākarikattōṭu caṅkilit toṭarpōl oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyatāyt tikaḻkiṟatu eṉṟu cintuveḷi akaḻ āyviṉait tiṟampaṭa naṭattiya ār.ṭi.pāṉarci1 kūṟukiṟār.
kiṟittava ūḻikkumuṉ koṟkaikku varum kappalkaḷ taṇporunai āṟṟiṉ mūlam ātittanallūr varai vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu intiya varalāṟṟut tuṟaip pērāciriyar tiru pālakiruṭṭiṇa nāyar2 karutukiṟār.
ivaikaḷiṉ mūlam cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tamiḻakattil āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭum, payirttoḻil vaḷarcci yāl peṟṟa nakara nākarikamum, kāḷaic ciṉṉamum koṟkai, ātitta nallūr, nallūr nākarikaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷai aṭuttē maturai, pūmpukār, kāñci, vañci mutaliya āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭu cintu āṟṟaṅkaraiyil malarnta mokañcatārō varai miḷirkiṉṟatu.
āriyarkaḷ intiya nāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭi eṭuttu vaikkum poḻutu, intiyāvil uyariya tirāviṭa nākarikam nilavi niṉṟatu. attirāviṭa nākarikam ekiptu, mecapottāmiyā ākiya veḷi nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta nākarikam eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu aṟiñar palar tirāviṭa nākarikam intiyāvilē arumpi ekiptu papilōṉ, ellam, cumēriyā, acīriyā, akkēṭiyā kirīṭ nāṭu kaḷil paraviyatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.3
makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, uṭal amaippu, elumpukaḷiṉ taṉmai ākiyavaikaḷai vaittu makkaḷiṉam iṉṟu pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. mokañcatārō, arappā, paluccicut tāṉattil uḷḷa nāl (Nal), ātittanallūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntukaṇṭa elumpukkūṭukaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷiṉiṉṟum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉ vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭai ōṭṭup puṭaippukaḷāl uḷaviyal kūṟukaḷai viḷakkamāka aṟiyamuṭiyumeṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭu aṭip paṭaiyil maṇṭaiōṭṭu amaippiyal āyvuttuṟaiyiṉ mūlam aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ yār eṉṟu muṭipukaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ātittanallūril vāḻnta makkaḷum, arappāvil vāḻnta makkaḷum kicu (Kish) nakaril vāḻnta makkaḷum āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷavarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ maṇṭai ōṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu āyntu kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. eliyaṭ cumit eṉṟa aṟiñariṉ āyviṉ paṭi ātittanallūril uḷḷa maṇṭaiyōṭu paḻaṅkāla ekittiya makkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭṭiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭatākap pirittuṇara muṭiyātatāka irukkiṟatu. villum, atti marattiṉ vaḻipāṭṭu marapum - oru niṟaivāṉa camayak kōṭpāṭṭumuṟai, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ kāṭarkaḷ teṉintiyak kāṭukaḷil uḷḷa ūrāḷikaḷiṭa miruntu peṟṟa naṉkoṭaikaḷāka aṟiñarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭṭa uṇmaikaḷākum. pāṉai caṭṭikaḷum avarkaḷ aḷitta naṉ koṭaikaḷēyākum. pūmarāṅ, teṉintiya ūtu kaṇaikkuḻal (Blow gun) kumukāyap poṟuppuk kaṭṭuppāṭu ākiyavaikaḷaik kulamarapuc ciṉṉa aṭippaṭaiyil koḷḷum vāḻkkai muṟai (Totemism)í« avarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟavaikaḷē eṉṟu mīṇṭum pativu ceytu koḷḷalām.1 aṭuttatāka, naṭunilaikkaṭal cārnta makkaḷai eṭuttuk koḷḷalām potuvāka intiya makkaḷuṭaiya - ciṟappākat teṉ intiya makkaḷuṭaiya uṭal ākka amaippum (Physical composition) kaṭalil kala mōṭṭum tiṟaṉum, payirt toḻiṟ kalaiyum matippuyarnta teṉ intiyap paṇpāṭum kiḻakku naṭunilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyiṉiṉṟu vantatākak koḷḷalām. teṉ intiya intuk kaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭu armīṉiya nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭṭai ottateṉṟu tarcuṭaṉ teṉ intiyāviṉ kulappirivu kaḷum, iṉamarapuk kuḻukkaḷum eṉṟa nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.2 tamiḻarkaḷiṭaiyē armīṉiya naṭunilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷiṉ iṇaippiṉ polivu peritum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñar palarum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
purucupuṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar āyvil, ātitta nallūril 1140-ēkkar aḷaviṟku mēl uḷḷa nilattil kāṇappaṭum putaikuḻikaḷil putiya kaṟkālattai aṭuttu arumpiya paḻaiya irumpuk kāla nākarikam nilaviyatu eṉṟu muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷār. ātittanallūril arumpiya tamiḻar nākarikam arappā nākarikattōṭum cumēriya, ellam, ekittu, papilōṉiya aciriya, nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu caṅkilit toṭar pōl iṇaintatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl muṭipu kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
ātittanallūril akaḻntukaṇṭa, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya poruḷkaḷaiyum tamiḻakattil uḷḷa nellai, maturai, irāmanātapuram, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu kaṇṭa putiya kaṟkāla, paḻaṅkaṟkāla, tol paḻaṅkaṟkālak karuvikaḷaiyum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal nilaṅkaḷiṉ nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu oppunōkki āyntu ekittiya cumēriya aciriya arappaṉ nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭum pārtta aṟiñarkaḷ tirāviṭa nākarikamē toṉmaiyāṉatu; ciṟappāṉatu; itiṉiṉṟu ulakil, pala nākarikaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. immakkaḷē ulaka nākarikattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ eṉṟum tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷē ekittilum ellattilum papilōṉilum kuṭiyēṟi irukka lām eṉṟum karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭu koṟkaip paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu iṇaintatu; arappā paṇpāṭṭōṭu neruṅkiya toṭar puṭaiyatu.
aṟiñarkaḷ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭai āḻntu aḻuttamāka ārāycci ceyya muṟpaṭṭāl potuvākat tamiḻar nākarikattaip paṟṟiyum ciṟappākak koṟkai nākarikattaip paṟṟiyum pala ariya - putiya uṇmaikaḷ veḷivarum.
ātittanallūril arumpiya ariya paṇpāṭṭiṉ ciṉṉaṅ kaḷum kōnakar koṟkaiyil koḻuntuviṭṭat tuḷirtteḻunta nakara - āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭum arappāvil aṟiñarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa nākarikattōṭu ottiruppatu āḻntu cintittup pārppataṟ kuriyatu. nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē uvariyaip paṟṟi naṭu nilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ naṉkaṟintirutṉar. cālamōṉ ñāṉi virumpi vēṇṭiyamaiyāl, el eṉṉum tēvaṉukkuk kaṭṭiya eḻil mikunta kōyilukku akil kaṭṭaiyum mayil iṟakum āṭṭu mayirum, kuraṅkum piṟavum pīṉiciyar tam kappalil vantu ēṟṟic ceṉṟuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu viviliyat tiru nūlil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. uvari, koṟkaikkut teṟkil uḷḷa paḻaiya tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamākum.

17. muṭivurai
aṟam ārntu aṇipeṟṟeḻunta arappāvum mokañca tārōvum āṭavalāṉum koṟṟavaiyum teṉmukak kaṭavuḷum tiruviḷaiyāṭal purinta tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ tiruviṭamākiya tirāviṭa intiyāviṟku iru kaṇkaḷ pōl viḷaṅkiṉa. tirāviṭa nākarikat tiṟku uyarnta eṭuttukkāṭṭāy ilaṅkiya iru perum nakaraṅkaḷum āriya arakkarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ nakara nākarikamum, avarkaḷatu ariya kāḷaic ciṉṉamum, kirāma nākarika mum, pacuc ciṉṉamum koṇṭa āriyarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēṉ? ulakiṟkē oru nantā viḷakkeṉa oḷiviṭṭōṅkiya cintu veḷit tirāviṭa nākarikamum tirāviṭap paṇpum tirāviṭa āṭciyum 4500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṭiyuṭaṉ vīḻntaṉa.
appāl ataṉ aṭivēril kiḷaitteḻunta tamiḻar āṭci yum avarkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷum mokalāyarkaḷāl vīḻttappaṭṭaṉa. tirāviṭarkaḷum, avarkaḷ vaḻivanta tamiḻarkaḷum vīram aṟṟa varkaḷ eṉṟō paṭaipalam illātavarkaḷ eṉṟō araciyal aṟivu peṟātavarkaḷ eṉṟō evarum kūṟamuṭiyātu. āṉāl avarkaḷ āṭci ēṉ aṭiyōṭu cāyntatu? paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum avarkaḷ āṭci ēṉ talaitūkkavillai? aḻintu pōṉa avarkaḷatu nakaraṅkaḷ ēṉ mīṇṭum talaitūkki eḻavillai? ivai namakkup putirāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ituvarai namatu nāṭṭu āṉṟōr kaḷum cāṉṟōrkaḷum aṟavōrkaḷum itaippaṟṟi āya muṉ varavillai; uṇmai kaṇṭu namakku vaḻikāṭṭa villai. ataṉāl āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻar kumukāyam viḻippuṟṟu eḻavillai. taṅkaḷ vīḻccikku uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷai aṟintu tuyil kaḷaintu mīṇṭu uyarvataṟkup pōrkkōlam pūṇṭeḻavillai.
cintu veḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ payirttoḻil ceytu aṟa vaḻiyil vāḻntārkaḷ; paṇpu niṟaintu niṉṟārkaḷ; nākarika vāḻvu naṭattivantaṉar; teyvattaip pēṇi vāḻntaṉar. āṉāl aṉṉiya nāṭṭiṉar ayal iṉattavarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ vāḻvaik kaṇṭu poṟāmai koṇṭu avarkaḷ aritiṉ muyaṉṟu kaṭṭiya nakaraṅ kaḷaiyum viḷai nilaṅkaḷaiyum, paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum cūṟaiyāṭi aḻittu aṭimaiyākka varuvārkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷ taṟpātukāp piṟkāṉa palamāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷ etuvum ceyyavillai. ākkiramikka varum ayalavarkaḷiṉ paṭaikaḷai aḻittu avarkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam varai viraṭṭi avarkaḷ nāṭṭait tākki avar kaḷuṭaiya paṭai kaḷaiyum, paṭaikkaruvikaḷaiyum aḻittu aṟivūṭṭu vataṟku ēṟṟa, tākkum paṭaikaḷai amaikkavillai, ataṉāl tirāviṭa intiyā vīḻntatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṟku aṭip paṭaiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutum tamiḻar nākarikamum kōnakar koṟkaiyum kūṭak kālavauttāl tāḻntatu; ayalavar paṭai yeṭuppāl, ākkira mippāl naintatu; iyaṟkaiyiṉ kōpattāl vīḻntatu.
arappāviṉ vīḻccikkuppiṉ tuḷirtta tamiḻnāṭum tamiḻ makkaḷum paṇṭaiya cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ pōl celvamum, nākarikamum aṟamum, neṟiyum, paṇpum oḻukkamum vāyntavarkaḷ. pakaivarkaḷait tākki viraṭṭi avarkaḷ nāṭuvaraip pōntu aṭittu vīḻtti avarkaḷukkum putti pukaṭṭum tiṟamai mikkavarkaḷ eṉṟālum cērar eṉṟum, cōḻar eṉṟum pāṇṭiyar eṉṟum mūṉṟu nāṭākap pirintatum mūvarum oruvarukkoruvar aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭu cakōtara nāṭukaḷaiyum araṇmaṉaikaḷaiyum vīṭukaḷai yum, viḷai nilaṅkaḷaiyum kalaikaḷaiyum, kalaik karuvikaḷaiyum aḻittu mūvaracarkaḷum palaṅkuṉṟi niṉṟamaiyāl mūvarum aṭiyōṭu vīḻa nērntatu. paḻam perum pāṇṭiyar āṭci koḷḷaiyaṉ camālutīṉ kaiyāl eḷitil vīḻnta nilaikaṇṭu pāṇṭiyakulam maṭṭumaṉṟi pāṇṭiyanāṭṭu makkaḷum, tamiḻ makkaḷum - ēṉ tirāviṭa iṉamum kaṇṇīr viṭātirukka muṭiyātu. pāṇṭiyanāṭu ellāt tamiḻarkaḷukkum tāyakam. avarkaḷ āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē cintuveḷi makkaḷōṭum, cīṉarkaḷōṭum cumēriyar kaḷōṭum ekittiyarkaḷōṭum vaṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭavarkaḷ. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āḻkaṭalkaḷaik kaṭantu cellum kalaṅkaḷaiyum kappaṟpaṭaikaḷaiyum kaṇṭavarkaḷ. imayam mutal īḻam varai ceṉṟu veṟṟiyīṭṭi imayattiṉ ucci yil kuṭaiyai nāṭṭiyavarkaḷ īḻam varai pōntu tirukkōṇa malai mītu koṭiyaip poṟittavarkaḷ; taṅkaḷatu valimai mikka kaṭaṟpaṭaiyiṉ tiṟattāl ciṅkaḷam, parmā, antamāṉ, nikkōpār, kaṭāram, malaiyam, ciṅkapuram, cāvakam, pālittīvu, tāylāntu, kampōcakam pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa nāṭukaḷai veṉṟu veṟṟik koṭiyaip paṟakka viṭṭavarkaḷ.
yavaṉarkaḷōṭum kirēkkarkaḷōṭum urōmarkaḷōṭum arapiyarkaḷōṭum cumēriyarkaḷōṭum, ekittiyarkaḷōṭum cīṉarkaḷōṭum vaṇikam naṭattiyavarkaḷ; āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kappaṟpaṭaiyiṉ tiṟattāl kaṭal kaṭantu yāṇṭum veṟṟi yīṭṭi, camayattaiyum kalaiyaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum ñālamel lām nākarikattaiyum parappiyavarkaḷ.
paḻamperum paṇpu vāynta, aṟaneṟi kaṇṭa tamiḻ makkaḷ mīṇṭun talaitūkkit taḻaittōṅka vēṇṭum. eṉavē, avarkaḷ mutalil tāṅkaḷ paṇpu vāynta nākarika makkaḷ eṉṟu uṇara vēṇṭum. appāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuḷ eḻunta iṉavēṟṟumai, kulavēṟṟumai, camaya vēṟṟumai aṉaittaiyum maṟantu oṉṟupaṭa vēṇṭum. appāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uyarvukkum urimaikkum pōriṭa vēṇṭum. nāṭu nalampeṟa - tirāviṭa makkaḷ vāḻvu malara - aṟivūṭṭuka kiḷarnteḻuka caṭṭam ceyka eṉṉum poṉ moḻiyai namatu mūla mantiramākak koṇṭu eḻuvārkaḷāka.

paṭaṅkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉum paṭṭattaraciyum
ippaṭam tirucci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa putukkōṭṭaikku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa aṇṇal vāyil aṟivaṉ kōyil mukamaṇṭa pattiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa tūṇil tīṭṭap peṟṟa oru oppaṟṟa eḻil ōviyamākum inta ōviyam pāṇṭiyaṉ avaṉīpa cēkara cīvallapaṉum avaṉatu araciyum ki.pi. (835-862) araca uṭaiyuṭaṉ aḻaku poliya niṟpatu pōl eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu - itu ki.pi. 9ām āṇṭil eḻutappeṟṟa neyvaṇṇa ōviyappaṭiyākum. cilaiyaiviṭa ōviyam aḻakuṭaṉ oḷirkiṟatu.
2. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuk koṭi
iraṇṭām caṭavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ I ilaṅkaiyai veṉṟa niṉaivukkuṟiyāka, avaṉāl tīṭṭap peṟṟa cācaṉattil caṭavarmaṉ vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ III”, īḻattai veṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa aracarkaḷil oruvaṉaik koṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭaikaḷaic ciṟaiyiṭṭu aracaṉ ēṟiccellum vaṇṭi, karuvūlam, aracu kaṭṭil, makuṭam, mālai pōṉṟa aracac ciṉṉaṅkaḷaik kaippaṟṟip pāṇṭiyaṉatu iru mīṉkaḷ tīṭṭiya koṭiyait tirukkōṇa malaiyil poṟittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. antak koṭiyiṉ uruvamē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum paṭamākum. tirukkōṇa malaiyi ṉiṉṟu ikkoṭi tīṭṭappaṭṭa malaippakuti uṭaikkappaṭṭu ulāntāk kārarkaḷ kaṭṭiya ceykaṇṭa piraṭarik kōṭṭaiyiṉ mītu vaittuk kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. (A.R.E.No. 356 of 1906 : 1-b. 19128 & 66) iṉṟu akkōṭṭai mītē koṭi uruvam kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
3. pāṇṭiya aracu muttirai
ukkiraṉ kōṭṭaic cācaṉattil poṟikkappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ ciriparāntakaṉ vīranārāyaṇaṉāl (ki.pi. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) tīṭṭap peṟṟa aracu muttirai. muttiraiyiṉ naṭuvil kīḻē villum mēlē ceṅkuttāy niṟkum nilaiyil iru mīṉkaḷum, arukē uṭkārntirukkum puliyum tīṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu cintikkat takkatu. cōḻar aracu ciṉṉattilum ivvāṟē mūṉṟu aracukaḷiṉ ciṉṉamum iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
4. pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉatu muttirai
inta muttirai pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracu muttiraiyaṉṟu. itu pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉ apirāma ativīra rāmapāṇṭiyaṉiṉ conta muttirai (ilāñcaṉam) ākum. inta iḷavaracaṉ caṭilavarmaṉ cīvallapaṉ eṉṟa ati vīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ makaṉ āvaṉ. ivar ki.pi. 1593-ām āṇṭu irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa iru ciṟṟūrkaḷai pārppaṉarkaḷukkut tāṉamāka aḷittāṉ. ataṟkāka avaṉāl eḻuti vaikkappaṭṭa ceppēṭṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa muttiraiyē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum muttiraiyākum. intac ceppēṭu ceṉṉait tol poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil uḷḷatu. (V.R. ii Ramnad 66 C.P.I of 1912).
5. aḵkacālai vināyakar kōyil
koṟkaiyil (maturōtaya nallūril) uḷḷa oru kuḷakkarai yil kāṇappaṭum kōyil aḵkacālai vināyakar kōyil eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itaṉaic civaṉ kōyil eṉṟum itu cōḻar pāṇiyil paḻaṅkālac cōḻarkaḷāl kaṭṭappeṟṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ik kōyiliṉ vimāṉam mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil uḷḷa cōḻarkaḷiṉ vimāṉa amaippaip pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar pāṇṭi yarkaḷ kaṭṭiya intac civaṉ kōyilai iṭaikkālattil cōḻarkaḷ māṟṟiyamait tirukkalām eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ cuvarkaḷil kalveṭṭukkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. atil aḵkacālai īcuvaramuṭaiyār kōyil eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
6. camaṇa tīrttaṅkarar cilai
koṟkai ūrai aṭaintatum vaḻiyil mutaṉ mutalākak kāṇappaṭuvatu intac camaṇamuṉivariṉ cilaiyē. itu koṟkai yil uḷḷa paḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. maṟṟoru tīrttaṅkarar cilai ūrukku aṇmaiyil uḷḷatu tōṭṭam oṉṟil uḷḷatu. iṅku kiṟittava ūḻiyai oṭṭi camaṇaneṟi celvākkup peṟ ṟiruntatu eṉṟu eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu. iṅkup pala camaṇa camayatta varkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvatil viyappillai.
7.(m) paḻam pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
iṅkup paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ purāṇak kācukaḷum erutu uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭa kācukaḷum, yāṉai uruvam poṟikkap paṭṭa kācukaḷum piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. aṉaittum cempiṉāl āṉavai. ivai koṟkai aḵkacālaiyil acciṭappaṭṭavai.
8. (ā) iṭaikālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
ivai iṭaikkālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷāka eṇṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil cu eṉṟa tamiḻ varivaṭivam tīṭṭap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟil karuṭaṉ, kaṇṭa, kōṭari, civaṉ-pārvati uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai koṟkaiyil acciṭap paṭṭavai.
9.(ï) iṟutik kālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
itil kāṇappaṭum kācukaḷ pāṇṭiya araciṉ iṟutikkālat til koṟkai aḵkacālaiyil acciṭap paṭṭavai. ivaikaḷil karuṭaṉ, tirumāl, iḷampiṟai kaṇṭa kōṭari, vaiṇavarkaḷiṉ ciṉṉamākiya nāmam, caṅku, cakkaram mutaliyavai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
10. ātitta nallūr pāṉai caṭṭikaḷ
iṅkuḷḷa pāṉai caṭṭikaḷ kaṟuppu - civappu vaṇṇam vāyntavaikaḷāy uḷḷaṉa. ivai palvēṟu vakaikaḷāyk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kai vaṇṇattai eṭuttukkāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
11. ātittanallūr eḵku āyutaṅkaḷ
iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum eḵkuk karuvikaḷ ki.mu. 100 mutal 
ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukku uṭpaṭṭa kālattavai eṉṟu karutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itil tiricūlam, vāḷ, īṭṭi, eḵku, ampumuṉaikaḷ, uḷi, irupuṟamum viḷimpuḷḷa vāḷ, mīṉeṟivēl, eṟivēl pōṉṟavai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
12. ātittanallūr veṇkalap poruṭkaḷ
veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya vēlaip pāṭukaḷ niṟainta pūntoṭṭikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, kiṇṇaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itil pala ceṭikaḷum, koṭikaḷum, pūkkaḷum, arumpu kaḷum, ilaikaḷum, vilaṅkukaḷum, paṟavaikaḷum aḻakuṟac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattil veṇkalak kālamum irumpuk kālamum taḷirttuviṭṭaṉa eṉpa taṟku ivai ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāy miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.

1.	“The evidence furnished by the Tamil classics of existence of an extensive chank-bangle industry in the extreme south of India during the height of ancient Tamil civilisation 1200 to 2000 years ago has received unexpected by conclusive corroboration within the present year (1912) through discoveries which I have made on the sites of the once famous Tamil cities of Korkai and Kayal (now Palaya Kayal). These cities are now represented by mounds of rubbish adjacent tovillages stillbearing the appelation of their celebrated predecessors.
	The greatest find was at Korkai which as already noted flourished from a date well antecedent to the christian era down to some indeterminate date prior to 1000 A.D. when the accretion of silt at the mouth of the Tambraparani drove the inhabitants to build another city (kayal) at the new mouth of the river. Here on the landward outskirt of the village I unearthed a five series of chank work shop waste, seventeen fragments in all. The whole numbers were found lying on the surface of the ground in a place where the old Pandyan coins have from time to time been discovered according to the information gathered in the village. “The Sacred chanks of India by James Hornell, F.L.S.” (1914) P. 45. Ibid P.46.

3.	The early writers of the church, however claim that the use of the fish to represent the Saviour was derived from a rebas taken from the Greek word for fish “ICHTHYS”, the letter of which are interpreted to mean Jesus Christ. God, Son, Saviour, One Calls Jesus “The Fisher of Men”; another says “Christ
	is figuratively called a fish”; another, “He is the fish that lives in the midst of water”, and still another “we small fish are like ICHTHYS, Our Jesus Christ born in water and saved only by remaining in water” - Decorative Motives of Oriental Art by Katherine M. Ball. P. 201 (1927) London.
1.	The fish had already appeared in Pagan art, but the christians soon discovered, that in Greek language the five letters constituting the word ‘fish’ were the initial letter of the phrase “Jesus Christ the son of God the Saviour” writing about A.D.200. On Baptism Tertuleian said “But we little fishes after the example of our fish Jesus Christ are born in water while Augustine (A.D.C. 425) said that in the word fish” Christ is mystically understood because he was able to live that is, to exist without sin the abyss of this mortality as in the depth of waters”. Thus the fish might stand symbolically for the name of Christ. In the catacomb of Priscilla there is an inscription reading “Alexandar in” after which a fish is shown Completing the phrase Alexandar in Christ. Or fishes might stand for the Christians themselves. A grave inscription of Licinia Amias from around A.D. 200 (See illustration) has word “Fish” followed by “of the living” which must mean “Jesus christ, the son God the saviour of living” and is adorned with to fishes which may represent the christian who are “the living”. The inilials D.M. which appear on this inscription stand for Dis Manibas meaning “To the sprihits of the world of the dead”. These initials appeard regularly on Pagan graves and were friquetly employed by christians simplyas
	a conventional form and without further thought of their Original meaning” - Light from the ancient past - Jack Finegan (1951) PP. 382-383.
1.	Of these princes Jatavarman Sundara Pandian 1, claims in his inscription to have conquered ceylon while Jatavarman Vira Pandia II, who took Elam A.D. 1253/4-1275) killed one of the two Kings, captured his army, charriots, treasures, throne, crown, necklace, bracelets, parasols, chaures, and other royal possessions planted the Pandian flag with the double fish on Konamalai and the high peaks of Trikutakiri mountain, received elephants as tribute from the other Kings of Ceylon subdued the Kerala* - (A.R.E.No 356 of 1906; ib 1912 & 65) - Ceylon coins and curreney - H.W.Codrington, B.A. (Oxon) C.C.S.; 1925.
2.	As said above in the south also we have a Minavan the Pandyan King of Madura. His lanchanam was also the two fish may be seen in the Ramnad Plates of Abhirama Ativerarama Pandya in the Madras Museum in the Pandyan coins in all the temples built by the Pandyas in Southern India and even on the jambs
	of the gate of the Frederick Fort at Trincomalee in Ceylon. Again he says that the horn fish of the flag of Greece is the same horn fish that was worshipped by the Pandya Minas Madura Kings” - The Minavan in Mohenjo - Daro - Rev. H. Heras, S.J.

1.	Physical features have been taken into account in determining racial elements. Prehistoric cronology can be studied from the finds at Adichanallur. Nal in Baluchistan and Mohenjo - Daro. It has been that the Mohenjo - Daro skulls of Proto - Australoid type are related to the skulls from Kish from Adichanallur and from those of the modern Veddhas. According to Elliot Smith an Adichanallur skull is indistinguishable from the early Egyptian type. The generally accepted theory is that the earliest inhabitants of the Peninsula were negroid in type akin to the Kadars and Uralis of South Indian forests and their contribution was probably the bow and the cult of the ficus tree - a fertility cult .... Next came the Mediteranian race. It is these that have been largely responsible for the physical composition of the peoples of Indian and especially South India and that have enriched South Indian culture Arts of agriculture & navigation came with them. These were from the eastern Mediteranean with them the Armenoids. - Origin and spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1971. P. 6.

	(b) Travancore Tribes and castes - L.A.Krishna Iyer Vol.II. P. 292-293.
	(c) The Ancient History of the Near East - H.R.Hall. P. 173, 174-212.
	(d) “The earliest stone age culture of India is represented by the hand axe technique of Madras, and the old stone age people may have migrated from South India into central India where in the Narbada valley, have been found middle pleistocene tools and a fauna gradually extended through the Ganges and Jamuna valley to North Western India right up to Himalayan Hills” - India and the Pacific World - Kalidas Nag. P. 15.
	The hypothesis that would represent what we know of their history most correctly would place their Original seat in the extreme South, some were probably not for from Madura or Tanjore and thence spreading fan-like towards the north. They have no traditions which point to any seat of race out of India or of their having emigrated from any Country whose inhabitants they can claim any kindred so far as they know they indigenous and oboriginal” - Tamilian Antiquary (No.1) - Dr. Fergusson.
	“Among the modern Indians, as amongst the modern Greeks or Italians the ancient Pre-Aryan type of the has (as the primitive of the alway does) survived, while that of the Aryan conqueror died out long ago and it is to this Dravidian ethnic type of India that the Sumerians bear most resemblance so far as we can judge from his monuments. He was very like a Southern Hindu of the Dekkan (who still speaks Dravidian languages) and it is by no means impossible that the Sumerians were an Indian race which passed certainly by land, perhaps also by sea, through Persia to the Valley of the two rivers. It was in the Indian home (perhaps Indus Valley) that their writing may have been invented and progressed from a purely pictorial to a simplified and abreviated form which afterwards in Babylonia took on its peculiat unifrom appearance owing to its being written within a aquare ended stilus on soft clay. On the way they left the seeds of culture in Elam ... ... there is little doubt that India must have been one of the earliest centres of civilization and it seems natural to suppose that the strange unsemitic uraryan people who came from the east to civilise the west were of Indian the Sumerians were in type” - An Ancient History of the Near East - Dr. R.Hall.
	The Madras presidency is the habitate of a Tamil Race whose civilization was the most ancient. A branch of Tamil Race spread a vast civilization on the Euphrates in very ancient times whose astrology, religion, love, morals and rites etc., furnished the foundation of the Asryian and Babylonian civilization and whose mythology was the source of the christian Bible. Another branch of them spread from the Malabar coast and gave rise to a wonderful Egyptian civilization and the Aryans are ir debted to the race in many respects. The colassal temples in South India proclaim the Engineering Art of the Tamil race” - Qutoed in “Prabuddha Bharatha” (Monthly Magazine) by Swami Vivekananda Sep. 1921.
